**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_source_data", "label_zh": "輸入網格或圖徵源", "label_en": "Input Raster or Feature Sources", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input source locations. This is a raster or feature (point, line, or polygon) identifying the cells or locations that will be used to calculate the least accumulated cost distance for each output cell location. For rasters, the input type can be integer or floating point."}, {"parameter": "in_barrier_data", "label_zh": "輸入網格和圖徵障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Raster or Feature Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Feature Layer", "description_en": "The dataset that defines the barriers. The barriers can be defined by an integer or a floating-point raster, or by a point, line, or polygon feature. For a raster barrier, the barrier must have a valid value, including zero, and the areas that are not barriers must be NoData."}, {"parameter": "in_surface_raster", "label_zh": "輸入表面網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Surface Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "A raster defining the elevation values at each cell location. The values are used to calculate the actual surface distance covered when passing between cells."}, {"parameter": "in_cost_raster", "label_zh": "輸入成本網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Cost Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "A raster defining the impedance or cost to move planimetrically through each cell. The value at each cell location represents the cost-per-unit distance for moving through the cell. Each cell location value is multiplied by the cell resolution while also compensating for diagonal movement to obtain the total cost of passing through the cell. The values of the cost raster can be integer or floating point. Cost raster values that are negative or zero are invalid but will be treated as small positive cost values."}, {"parameter": "in_vertical_raster", "label_zh": "輸入垂直網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Vertical Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "A raster defining the z-values for each cell location. The values are used for calculating the slope used to identify the vertical factor incurred when moving from one cell to another."}, {"parameter": "vertical_factor", "label_zh": "垂直系數 （可選）", "label_en": "Vertical Factor (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Vertical Factor", "description_en": "Specifies the relationship between the vertical cost factor and the vertical relative moving angle (VRMA). There are several factors with modifiers that identify a defined vertical factor graph. Additionally, a table can be used to create a custom graph. The graphs are used to identify the vertical factor used in calculating the total cost for moving into a neighboring cell. In the option descriptions below, two acronyms are used: VF stands for vertical factor, which defines the vertical difficulty encountered in moving from one cell to the next; VRMA stands for vertical relative moving angle, which identifies the slope angle between the FROM or processing cell and the TO cell. The options are as follows: Binary—If the VRMA is greater than the low-cut angle and less than the high-cut angle, the VF is set to the value associated with the zero factor; otherwise, it is infinity. Linear—The VF is a linear function of the VRMA. Symmetric Linear—The VF is a linear function of the VRMA in either the negative or positive side of the VRMA, and the two linear functions are symmetrical with respect to the VF (y) axis. Inverse Linear—The VF is an inverse linear function of the VRMA. Symmetric Inverse Linear—The VF is an inverse linear function of the VRMA in either the negative or positive side of the VRMA, and the two linear functions are symmetrical with respect to the VF (y) axis. Cos—The VF is the cosine-based function of the VRMA. Sec—The VF is the secant-based function of the VRMA. Cos-Sec—The VF is the cosine-based function of the VRMA when the VRMA is negative and is the secant-based function of the VRMA when the VRMA is not negative. Sec-Cos—The VF is the secant-based function of the VRMA when the VRMA is negative and is the cosine-based function of the VRMA when the VRMA is not negative. Hiking Time—The VF is the hiking time function of the VRMA. Bidirectional Hiking Time—The VF is a bidirectional modified hiking time function of the VRMA. Table—A table file will be used to define the vertical-factor graph that is used to determine the VFs. The modifiers to the vertical keywords are the following: Zero factor—The vertical factor used when the VRMA is zero. This factor positions the y-intercept of the specified function. By definition, the zero factor is not applicable to any of the trigonometric vertical functions (COS, SEC, COS-SEC, or SEC-COS). The y-intercept is defined by these functions. Low Cut angle—The VRMA angle below which the VF will be set to infinity. High Cut angle—The VRMA angle above which the VF will be set to infinity. Slope—The slope of the straight line used with the Linear and Inverse Linear vertical factor keywords. The slope is specified as a fraction of rise over run (for example, 45 percent slope is 1/45, which is input as 0.02222). Table name—The name of the table defining the VF."}, {"parameter": "in_horizontal_raster", "label_zh": "輸入水平網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Horizontal Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "A raster defining the horizontal direction at each cell. The values on the raster must be integers ranging from 0 to 360, with 0 degrees being north, or toward the top of the screen, and increasing clockwise. Flat areas should be given a value of -1. The values at each location will be used in conjunction with the Horizontal Factor parameter to determine the horizontal cost incurred when moving from a cell to its neighbors."}, {"parameter": "horizontal_factor", "label_zh": "水平系數 （可選）", "label_en": "Horizontal Factor (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Horizontal Factor", "description_en": "Specifies the relationship between the horizontal cost factor and the horizontal relative moving angle (HRMA). There are several factors with modifiers that identify a defined horizontal factor graph. Additionally, a table can be used to create a custom graph. The graphs are used to identify the horizontal factor used in calculating the total cost of moving into a neighboring cell. In the option descriptions below, two acronyms are used: HF stands for horizontal factor, which defines the horizontal difficulty encountered when moving from one cell to the next; HRMA stands for horizontal relative moving angle, which identifies the angle between the horizontal direction from a cell and the moving direction. The options are as follows: Binary—If the HRMA is less than the cut angle, the HF is set to the value associated with the zero factor; otherwise, it is infinity. Forward—Only forward movement is allowed. The HRMA must be greater than or equal to 0 and less than 90 degrees (0 <= HRMA < 90). If the HRMA is greater than 0 and less than 45 degrees, the HF for the cell is set to the value associated with the zero factor. If the HRMA is greater than or equal to 45 degrees, the side value modifier value is used. The HF for any HRMA equal to or greater than 90 degrees is set to infinity. Linear—The HF is a linear function of the HRMA. Inverse Linear—The HF is an inverse linear function of the HRMA. Table—A table file will be used to define the horizontal factor graph used to determine the HFs. The modifiers to the horizontal factors are the following: Zero factor—The horizontal factor to be used when the HRMA is zero. This factor positions the y-intercept for any of the horizontal factor functions. Cut angle—The HRMA angle beyond which the HF will be set to infinity. Slope—The slope of the straight line used with the Linear and Inverse Linear horizontal factor keywords. The slope is specified as a fraction of rise over run (for example, 45 percent slope is 1/45, which is input as 0.02222). Side value—The HF when the HRMA is greater than or equal to 45 degrees and less than 90 degrees when the Forward horizontal factor keyword is specified. Table name—The name of the table defining the HF."}, {"parameter": "out_back_direction_raster", "label_zh": "輸出反向網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Back Direction Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The back direction raster contains the calculated direction in degrees. The direction identifies the next cell along the shortest path back to the closest source while avoiding barriers. The range of values is from 0 degrees to 360 degrees, with 0 reserved for the source cells. Due east is 90, and the values increase clockwise (180 is south, 270 is west, and 360 is north). The output raster is of type float."}, {"parameter": "out_source_direction_raster", "label_zh": "輸出源方向網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Source Direction Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The source direction raster identifies the direction of the least accumulated cost source cell as an azimuth in degrees. The range of values is from 0 degrees to 360 degrees, with 0 reserved for the source cells. Due east is 90, and the values increase clockwise (180 is south, 270 is west, and 360 is north). The output raster is of type float."}, {"parameter": "out_source_location_raster", "label_zh": "輸出源位置網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Source Location Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The source location raster is a multiband output. The first band contains a row index, and the second band contains a column index. These indexes identify the location of the source cell that is the least accumulated cost distance away."}, {"parameter": "source_initial_accumulation", "label_zh": "初始累積 （可選）", "label_en": "Initial Accumulation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double; Field", "description_en": "The initial accumulative cost that will be used to begin the cost calculation. Allows for the specification of the fixed cost associated with a source. Instead of starting at a cost of zero, the cost algorithm will begin with this value. The values must be zero or greater. The default is 0."}, {"parameter": "source_maximum_accumulation", "label_zh": "最大累積 （可選）", "label_en": "Maximum Accumulation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double; Field", "description_en": "The maximum accumulation for the traveler for a source. The cost calculations continue for each source until the specified accumulation is reached. The values must be greater than zero. The default accumulation is to the edge of the output raster."}, {"parameter": "source_cost_multiplier", "label_zh": "成本乘數 （可選）", "label_en": "Costs Multiplier (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double; Field", "description_en": "The multiplier that will be applied to the cost values. This allows for control of the mode of travel or the magnitude at a source. The greater the multiplier, the greater the cost to move through each cell. The values must be greater than zero. The default is 1."}, {"parameter": "source_direction", "label_zh": "行駛方向 （可選）", "label_en": "Travel Direction (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String; Field", "description_en": "Specifies the direction of the traveler when applying horizontal and vertical factors. If you select the String option, you can choose between from and to options, which will be applied to all sources. If you select the Field option, you can select the field from the source data that determines the direction to use for each source. The field must contain the text string FROM_SOURCE or TO_SOURCE. Travel from source—The horizontal factor and vertical factor will be applied beginning at the input source and travel out to the nonsource cells. This is the default. Travel to source—The horizontal factor and vertical factor will be applied beginning at each nonsource cell and travel back to the input source."}, {"parameter": "distance_method", "label_zh": "距離法 （可選）", "label_en": "Distance Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the distance will be calculated using a planar (flat earth) or a geodesic (ellipsoid) method. Planar—The distance calculation will be performed on a projected flat plane using a 2D Cartesian coordinate system. This is the default. Geodesic—The distance calculation will be performed on the ellipsoid. Regardless of input or output projection, the results will not change."}]

**signatures**　
- DistanceAccumulation(in_source_data, {in_barrier_data}, {in_surface_raster}, {in_cost_raster}, {in_vertical_raster}, {vertical_factor}, {in_horizontal_raster}, {horizontal_factor}, {out_back_direction_raster}, {out_source_direction_raster}, {out_source_location_raster}, {source_initial_accumulation}, {source_maximum_accumulation}, {source_cost_multiplier}, {source_direction}, {distance_method})
- VfBinary({zeroFactor}, {lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle})
- VfLinear({zeroFactor}, {lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {slope})
- VfInverseLinear({zeroFactor}, {lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {slope})
- VfSymLinear({zeroFactor}, {lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {slope})
- VfSymInverseLinear({zeroFactor}, {lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {slope})
- VfCos({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {cosPower})
- VfSec({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {secPower})
- VfCosSec({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {cosPower}, {secPower})
- VfSecCos({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle}, {secPower}, {cos_power})
- VfHikingTime({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle})
- VfBidirHikingTime({lowCutAngle}, {highCutAngle})
- VfTable(inTable)
- HfBinary({zeroFactor}, {cutAngle})
- HfForward({zeroFactor}, {sideValue})
- HfLinear({zeroFactor}, {cutAngle}, {slope})
- HfInverseLinear({zeroFactor}, {cutAngle}, {slope})
- HfTable(inTable)

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_source_data、in_barrier_data、in_surface_raster、in_cost_raster、in_vertical_raster、vertical_factor、in_horizontal_raster、horizontal_factor、out_back_direction_raster、out_source_direction_raster、out_source_location_raster、source_initial_accumulation、source_maximum_accumulation、source_cost_multiplier、source_direction、distance_method。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 010159：無法打開 <value> 文件 <value>。 · 描述

**id**　cf92de51a50d406976-1

**english**　010159: Unable to open <value> file of <value>.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 10101-10200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10151-10175-010159.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10151-10175-010159.html

**text**　嘗試訪問指定文件時出錯。 通常，這可能是因為路徑錯誤或無權訪問資料文件夾。 如果路徑和權限正確，則下一個可能的問題來源可能是文件已損壞。
此外，還可能在執行某些 Spatial Analyst 操作時看見此錯誤，這些操作要求輸入網格具有某些統計屬性。 以下情況可能導致指定資料集無效：
尚未計算統計資料。
統計資料包含大于 1 的跳躍因子。
統計資料包含忽略值。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10151-10175-010159.html#description

## 010159：無法打開 <value> 文件 <value>。 · 解決方案

**id**　cf92de51a50d406976-2

**english**　010159: Unable to open <value> file of <value>.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 10101-10200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10151-10175-010159.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10151-10175-010159.html

**text**　檢查您是否已正確指定文件并且您具有讀取權限。 如果已執行此操作并且問題仍然存在，接下來應確定文件是否有效。 嘗試使用可以讀取該格式的應用程序查看文件內容。 如果不可行，則表示文件可能已損壞，需要重新創建。
如果輸入網格資料集的統計資料出于任何列出的原因無效，則使用計算統計資料工具計算資料集的適當統計資料。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10151-10175-010159.html#solution

## 刪除資料庫序列 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　cfa177603638a91c22-2

**english**　Delete Database Sequence

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 地理資料庫管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/delete-database-sequence.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/delete-database-sequence.html

**text**　此工具可用于文件地理資料庫、移動地理資料庫以及 IBM Db2、Microsoft SQL Server、Oracle、PostgreSQL 和 SAP HANA 中的企業級地理資料庫。
可使用 ListDatabaseSequences 函數獲取文件地理資料庫中的資料庫序列列表。 這樣可以在刪除序列之前確認序列名稱并查看序列屬性。
在企業級地理資料庫中，資料庫序列存儲由資料庫平臺處理。 有關詳細信息，請參閱各個 RDBMS 平臺文檔。 可以使用集成環境來查看序列。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/delete-database-sequence.html#usage

- 要從中刪除序列的文件地理資料庫或移動地理資料庫位置的完整路徑，或用于連接到要從中刪除序列的企業級地理資料庫的資料庫連接文件 (.sde)。 在資料庫連接中指定的用戶必須在資料庫中具有以下權限： Db2—DBADM 權限 Oracle—必須是序列所有者或具有 DROP ANY SEQUENCE 系統權限 PostgreSQL—必須是序列所有者 SAP HANA—必須是 標準 用戶 SQL Server—在存儲序列的資料庫方案中具有 ALTER OR CONTROL 權限
- Workspace

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/delete-database-sequence.html#derived-output

## 刪除資料庫序列 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 地理資料庫管理工具集

**english**　Delete Database Sequence

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/delete-database-sequence.html

**id**　cfa177603638a91c22-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/delete-database-sequence.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Delete Database Sequence，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入工作空間 [in_workspace]、序列名稱 [seq_name]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/delete-database-sequence.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_workspace", "label_zh": "輸入工作空間", "label_en": "Input Workspace", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Workspace", "description_en": "The full path to the location of the file or mobile geodatabase from which you want to delete a sequence or the database connection file (.sde) to connect to the enterprise geodatabase from which you want to delete a sequence. The user specified in the database connection must have the following permissions in the database: Db2—DBADM authority Oracle—Must be the sequence owner or have the DROP ANY SEQUENCE system privilege PostgreSQL—Must be the sequence owner SAP HANA—Must be a standard user SQL Server—ALTER OR CONTROL permission on the database schema where the sequence is stored"}, {"parameter": "seq_name", "label_zh": "序列名稱", "label_en": "Sequence Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the database sequence you want to delete. Once deleted, the sequence cannot be used to generate sequence IDs when called from existing custom applications or expressions."}]

**signatures**

## 110129: 評估“最佳聚類數”可能會花費大量時間。 · 描述

**id**　cfb3b35e3d8b942a25-1

**english**　110129: The evaluation of the Optimal Number of Clusters can take a significant amount of time.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110101-110200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110126-110150-110129.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110126-110150-110129.html

**text**　當您將 聚類數 參數留空時，該工具將為您評估最佳聚類數。該工具將求解 2、3、4 或最多 15 個聚類。這一過程需要相當的時間，特別是當 分析欄位 較多或圖徵數較大時。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110126-110150-110129.html#description

## 110129: 評估“最佳聚類數”可能會花費大量時間。 · 解決方案

**id**　cfb3b35e3d8b942a25-2

**english**　110129: The evaluation of the Optimal Number of Clusters can take a significant amount of time.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110101-110200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110126-110150-110129.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110126-110150-110129.html

**text**　使用一些其他策略來決定創建多少聚類。一旦確定了固定圖徵集的最佳聚類數、 分析欄位 以及特定的 空間約束 ，您就不再需要重復這一評估步驟了。但是，如果更改了 分析欄位 、 空間約束 或 輸入圖徵 ，最佳聚類數（由 偽 F 統計量 決定）可能也會改變。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110126-110150-110129.html#solution

**id**　cfd041fe2e9544249a-2

- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 160701-160800

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160701-160725-160723.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160701-160725-160723.html

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 橡皮頁變換圖徵 (編輯工具) · 匯總

**id**　cfdf69fbeeab2c2703-1

**english**　Rubbersheet Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 編輯工具箱
- 合并工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/rubbersheet-features.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/rubbersheet-features.html

**text**　利用指定的橡皮頁變換鏈接，通過橡皮頁變換對輸入圖徵進行空間調整修改，從而使輸入圖徵更好地與所需目標圖徵對齊。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/editing/rubbersheet-features.html#summary

## 橡皮頁變換圖徵 (編輯工具) · 注意：

**id**　cfdf69fbeeab2c2703-2

**english**　Rubbersheet Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 編輯工具箱
- 合并工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/rubbersheet-features.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/rubbersheet-features.html

**text**　此工具會修改輸入資料。 有關詳細信息以及避免意外更改資料的策略，請參閱修改或更新輸入資料的工具。
該工具將在生成橡皮頁變換鏈接工具之后使用。 橡皮頁變換根據指定的橡皮頁變換連接線進行空間校正，從而使輸入圖徵位置更加準確的與目標圖徵位置對齊。 輸入鏈接圖徵表示常規鏈接；輸入點圖徵表示在橡皮頁變換過程中保持源位置不動的標識鏈接。 輸入鏈接圖徵與標識鏈接圖徵均必須具有 SRC_FID 與 TGT_FID 欄位。 注: 所有輸入必須處于同一座標系。
方法參數確定用于在橡皮頁變換過程中創建臨時 TIN 的插值方法。 線性—該方法用于創建快速的 TIN 表面，但并不真正考慮鄰域。 此方法速度稍快，并且當許多橡皮頁變換鏈接均勻分布在要調整的資料上時，將產生優質結果。 自然鄰域法—該方法稍慢，但當橡皮頁變換連接線不是很多并且在資料集中較為分散時，得出的結果會更加精確。 在這種情況下使用線性方法不夠精確。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/editing/rubbersheet-features.html#usage

**text**　"""Name:        RubbersheetFeatures_example_script2.py
Description: Performs rubbersheeting spatial adjustment using links produced by
             GenerateRubbersheetLinks, assuming newly updated roads are more
             accurate than existing base roads. The links go from base road data
             to corresponding newly updated road data. The links are then
             analyzed for potential errors. They are then used to adjust the
             base roads (a copy is made) to better align with the updated roads.
"""

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.overwriteOutput = True
arcpy.env.workspace = r"D:\conflationTools\ScriptExamples\data.gdb"

# Set local variables
sourceFeatures = "baseRoads"
targetFeatures = "updateRoads"
grlOutput = "grlinks_out"
grlOutputPts = "grlinks_out_pnt"

search_distance = "300 Feet"
match_fields = "FULLNAME RD_NAME"

qaLocations = "qa_locations"

# Generate rubbersheet links
arcpy.edit.GenerateRubbersheetLinks(sourceFeatures, targetFeatures, grlOutput, search_distance, match_fields)

"""
Note 1:  The result of GenerateRubbersheetLinks may contain errors; see the tool reference.
         Inspection and editing may be necessary to ensure correct links before using
         them for rubbersheeting.

         One of the common errors is intersecting or touching links. Their locations
         can be found by the process below.
"""

# Find locations where links intersect or touch. The result contains coincident points.
arcpy.analysis.Intersect(grlOutput, qaLocations, "", "", "POINT")

# Delete coincident points
arcpy.management.DeleteIdentical(qaLocations, "Shape")

"""
Note 2:  You can manually inspect locations in qaLocations and delete or
         modify links as needed.
"""

# Make a copy of the sourceFeatures for rubbersheeting
arcpy.management.CopyFeatures(sourceFeatures, "sourceFeatures_Copy")

# Use the links for rubbersheeting
arcpy.edit.RubbersheetFeatures("sourceFeatures_Copy", grlOutput, grlOutputPts, "LINEAR")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/editing/rubbersheet-features.html#derived-output

**chapter**

**id**　cfdf69fbeeab2c2703-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/rubbersheet-features.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Rubbersheet Features，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵 [in_features]、輸入鏈接圖徵 [in_link_features]、輸入點圖徵作為標識鏈接 （可選） [in_identity_links]、方法 （可選） [method]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

## 創建廊道 · 什么是廊道？

**id**　cfe49a97026a7154f5-3

**english**　Creating a corridor

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 距離分析工具集
- 舊版本
- 舊版本距離分析工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html#170

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html

**text**　廊道工具返回一個網格，其中對于每個像元位置，計算兩個輸入累積成本網格的成本距離之和（累積成本）。 兩個網格成本的總和為每個像元位置確定了從一個源到另一個源并經過該像元位置的最小成本路徑。
廊道工具與成本路徑工具相關，后者僅返回具有最低成本路徑的輸出網格。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html#170

## 創建廊道 · 為什么要找到成本最低的廊道？

**id**　cfe49a97026a7154f5-4

**english**　Creating a corridor

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 距離分析工具集
- 舊版本

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html

**text**　您可以使用廊道工具而不是成本路徑工具來連接保護計劃中的兩塊鹿棲息地，并希望為鹿保護最佳廊道，而不是僅僅緩衝一條路徑。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html#63D

## 創建廊道 · 創建廊道

**id**　cfe49a97026a7154f5-5

**english**　Creating a corridor

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 距離分析工具集
- 舊版本
- 舊版本距離分析工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html#0F5

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html

**text**　要創建廊道，必須使用成本距離工具（例如成本距離或路徑距離）創建兩個成本累積網格，每個網格對應一個源（或一組源）。 下圖顯示了出于演示目的從單個單元格位置創建的成本表面。 該程序實際上發生在輸入網格上的每個像元位置。
然后，廊道工具會將兩個累積成本表面相加在一起。
輸出網格未在兩個源之間識別一條最低成本路徑。該網格可標識源之間的累積成本范圍。 也就是說，到達源 1 的最低累積成本加上到達源 2 的最低累積成本等于通過某個像元的路徑的總累積成本。 如果從源 1 到源 2 的路徑經過該像元，則該累積成本就是最低累積成本。
如果從廊道網格中選擇值小于最大累積距離（或閾值）的所有像元，則生成的輸出網格將對應于不超過指定成本的像元帶（或廊道）。 由此產生的閾值輸出可以看作是像元的最小成本廊道，而不是最低成本路徑（單行）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html#0F5

## 創建廊道 · 替代方法

**id**　cfe49a97026a7154f5-6

**english**　Creating a corridor

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 距離分析工具集
- 舊版本
- 舊版本距離分析工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html#986

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html

**text**　將廊道限制在指定閾值的另一種方法是通過條件說明。 下面是使用不同工具實現此功能的幾個示例。
通過以下設置使用條件函數工具（來自“條件分析”工具集）：
輸入條件網格資料：accum_cor
表達式："Value < 100"
輸入條件為真時所取的網格資料或常量值：100
輸入條件為假時所取的網格資料或常量值：0
輸出網格：corr_thresh
使用具有以下設置的按屬性提取工具（來自“提取”工具集）：
輸入網格：accum_cor
Where 子句："Value < 100"
輸出網格：corr_thresh
使用具有以下設置的條件測試工具（來自“數學”>“邏輯”工具集）：
輸入網格：accum_cor
Where 子句："Value < 100"
輸出網格：corr_thresh
如果將多個源輸入成本距離工具以創建廊道工具的輸入累積成本網格，則根據源之間的累積成本值和指定的閾值，可能會產生多個廊道（每個源之間一個）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/creating-a-corridor.html#986

**id**　d01770492c392923fa-1

**english**　Sun Shadow Volume

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 可見性工具集

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html

**text**　用于創建閉合體，該閉合體可以使用給定日期和時間的陽光來建模每個輸入圖徵投射的陰影。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html#summary

## 太陽陰影體 (3D Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　d01770492c392923fa-2

**english**　Sun Shadow Volume

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 可見性工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html

**text**　所有輸入圖徵都應位于同一區域，因為相對太陽位置的計算基于第一個圖徵在第一個圖徵類中的位置。
如果太陽在給定的日期和時間不可見，或者如果太陽的相對位置與輸入圖徵成 90 度垂直角，則不會生成陰影體。
通過向太陽光的方向拉伸輸入圖徵，陰影將被模擬成一個閉合多面體。 光線均視為平行光線并且按太陽的相對位置計算出的方向進行傳播。每個陰影體都在一個垂直于太陽光線水平投影的垂直平面開始和結束。
以下欄位將歸因于陰影體圖徵： SOURCE—投射陰影體的圖徵類的名稱。 SOURCE_ID—投射陰影體的圖徵的唯一 ID。 DATE_TIME—用于計算太陽位置的當地日期和時間。 AZIMUTH—正北方向與太陽相對位置向下到地球地平線的垂直投影之間的角度（以度為單位）。 其值的范圍為 0 到 360 之間。 VERT_ANGLE—地平線與太陽相對位置之間的角度（以度為單位），其中 0 度表示太陽處于地平線的位置，90 度表示太陽位于地平線的正上方。 注: 通常，每個陰影體都將緊靠或緊貼其投影源圖徵。 如果無法以這種方式生成陰影，將會從圖徵的外部范圍邊界處進行創建。 如果以這種方式至少創建了一個陰影，則會包括一個名為 HUGS_FEATR 的欄位，該欄位會指示哪些陰影緊靠其對應圖徵。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html#usage

- 結束日期和時間 （可選）

- Date

## 太陽陰影體 (3D Analyst 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　d01770492c392923fa-24

**english**　Sun Shadow Volume

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 可見性工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html

**text**　'''*********************************************************************
Name: Model Shadows For GeoVRML Models
Description: Creates a model of the shadows cast by GeoVRML models
             imported to a multipatch feature class for a range of dates
             and times. A range of times from the start time and end
             time can also be specified by setting the EnforceTimes
             Boolean to True. This sample is designed to be used in a
             script tool.
*********************************************************************'''
# Import system modules
import arcpy
from datetime import datetime, time, timedelta

#*************************  Script Variables  **************************
inFiles = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(0) # list of input features
spatialRef = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(1) # list of GeoVRML files
outFC = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(2) # multipatch from 3D files
inTimeZone = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(3) # time zone
startDate = arcpy.GetParameter(4) # starting date as datetime
endDate = arcpy.GetParameter(5) # ending date as datetime
dayInterval = arcpy.GetParameter(6) # day interval as long (0-365)
minInterval = arcpy.GetParameter(7) # minute interval as long (0-60)
enforceTime = arcpy.GetParameter(8) # minute interval as Boolean
outShadows = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(9) # output shadow models
outIntersection = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(10) # shadow & bldg intersection

# Function to find all possible date/time intervals for shadow modelling
def time_list():
    dt_result = [startDate]
    if dayInterval:
        if endDate: #Defines behavior when end date is supplied
            while startDate < endDate:
                startDate += timedelta(days=dayInterval)
                dt_result.append(startDate)
            dt_result.append(endDate)
        else: # Behavior when end date is not given
            daymonthyear = datetime.date(startDate)
            while startDate <= datetime(daymonthyear.year, 12, 31, 23, 59):
                startDate += timedelta(days=dayInterval)
                dt_result.append(startDate)
    return dt_result

importFC = arcpy.CreateUniqueName('geovrml_import', 'in_memory')

# Import GeoVRML files to in-memory feature
arcpy.ddd.Import3DFiles(inFiles, importFC, 'ONE_FILE_ONE_FEATURE',
                        spatialRef, 'Z_IS_UP', 'wrl')

# Ensure that building models are closed
arcpy.ddd.EncloseMultiPatch(importFC, outFC, 0.05)

# Discard in-memory feature
arcpy.management.Delete(importFC)
dt_result = time_list()
for dt in dt_result:
    if dt == dt_result[0]:
        shadows = outShadows
    else:
        shadows = arcpy.CreateUniqueName('shadow', 'in_memory')
    arcpy.ddd.SunShadowVolume(outFC, dt, shadows, 'ADJUST_FOR_DST',
                              inTimeZone, '', minInterval, 'MINUTES')
    if dt is not dt_result[0]:
        arcpy.management.Append(shadows, outShadows)
        arcpy.management.Delete(shadows)
arcpy.ddd.Intersect3D(outFC, outIntersection, outShadows, 'SOLID')

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html#parameters

## 太陽陰影體 (3D Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 可見性工具集

**english**　Sun Shadow Volume

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html

**id**　d01770492c392923fa-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Sun Shadow Volume，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵 [in_features]、起始日期和時間 [start_date_and_time]、輸出圖徵類 [out_feature_class]、按夏令時調整 （可選） [adjusted_for_dst]、時區 （可選） [time_zone]、結束日期和時間 （可選） [end_date_and_time]、迭代間隔 （可選） [iteration_interval]、迭代單位 （可選） [iteration_unit]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/sun-shadow-volume.html#parameters
